# MEMORY.md — Live State & Behavior Log

> **READ THIS FILE FIRST**, before `CLAUDE.md`, before any action.
> This file holds live project state and a running log of behavior/functionality changes.
> **Whenever Jason asks for new behavior or functionality from me, append it to the BEHAVIOR LOG below and update the relevant state.**

---

## HOW TO USE THIS FILE

1. On session start: read this file top to bottom, then read `CLAUDE.md`.
2. Before acting: check "Active work" and "Open decisions" so you resume correctly (don't re-derive or re-scope settled things).
3. When Jason requests new behavior/functionality: add a dated entry to the **BEHAVIOR LOG** and update state sections as needed.
4. Keep this file current — it is the source of truth for _where things are_.

---

- **v1.224.0 — fuel's priced figures ride 30 days, not 90 (2026-09-16).** Jason: "Fuel page should be using rolling 30 days where it makes sense. A rolling 90 days is too long with fuel being so volatile." Prod proved it: 41 fills since May 24; $/gal 30-day $5.45 vs 90-day $4.75 vs lifetime $4.68 (Jun $4.16 → Sep $5.61), i.e. the 90-day blend quoted ~$0.70/gal under the pump. `fuelEconomy.FUEL_WINDOW_DAYS = 30`; fields renamed `costPerMile30 / mpg30 / avgCostPerGallon30 / windows30` so every label had to be revisited (Fuel answering line, Last Tank card, Fleet tab cost-to-run, truck page, load detail estimate, Guide ×4); new `paidPerGallon30` (every gallon bought in 30 days, partials and all) feeds the you-vs-national card and the answering line — that card had compared a LIFETIME $/gal to the EIA's current week, which stamps UNDER THE NATIONAL every time diesel climbs. MPG stays lifetime (mechanical, not priced). `avgWeeklyCost90` deleted: no consumer (the cash-flow board has its own `weeklyFuelCost30` since 2026-09-06, so the cash job and the rate job now agree). The rate ladder reads NO live fuel (Settings' cost assumptions), so break-even did not move; the truck/fleet cost-to-run did.

**SESSION 2026-09-13 — Agencies: the grain the freight bills prove (Agencies Nod Sheet, all leans nodded; settlement-only history = 2026 only).** Facts (memory `landstar-agency-model-findings.md`): the "Sent From" header of a Landstar freight bill prints `Posting Code` (the desk that posted), `Agency Name: {legal name} - {AGENCY code}`, `Contact Name`. A **Landstar agency owns a SET of posting codes**: its own code (the shared desk the owner posts from), each agent's own code, and older desks (Brian Williams posted SUU in Jan, SUH since Jul — both Momentum · JVL). dash's `brokers` were posting codes mislabeled as agencies: Central Pennsylvania Logistics · CPL was MAM + CJY + CPL, Momentum · JVL was SUH + JXO + JVL. 61 of 69 bills parse (8 are the older terminal-copy layout / misfiled load confirmations — station code only); `settlement_lines.agent_code` IS the posting code. Extracted headers: session scratchpad `freight-bill-headers.psv` (regenerate: `pdftotext -layout` on `/data/dts-docs/operations/loads/**/*-freight-bill.pdf` on dts-srv).

- **Process lessons:** (1) **dry-run every data migration on a restored copy of prod** (`pg_dump -Fc` → local Postgres.app 18 with a `extensions` schema for uuid-ossp/pgcrypto, `pg_restore -n public`, `psql --single-transaction -f`) — it caught `ON COMMIT DROP` temp tables, a PL/pgSQL alias/record clash, and the `loads.broker_id NOT NULL` blocker; simulate old-code and new-code writes for the deploy window. (2) Renames ship as expand (migration before merge) + contract (after Railway is live). (3) Backups: `~/dash-backups/dash-prod-YYYYMMDD-HHMM.dump`. (4) Subagents on Opus; parallel worktrees `~/projects/dash-pr{A,B}`; the second PR rebases onto the first.

**SESSION 2026-09-12 → 09-13 — Relationships v2 (REL-01 v2.0 nurture model), four PRs v1.212.0 → v1.215.0.** Doctrine (memory `rel-01-v2-nurture-model.md`): five DERIVED buckets — Tier 1/2/3 = established (≥3 delivered) graded by avg all-in gross RPM vs the LIVE ladder (≥Target · ≥Minimum · ≥Walk-away · below = losing money), Prospects, Parked (explicit or dormant = nothing two-way in 180 d); tiers are OWNER-set with a written reason → `agent_tier_history`; **everything is manual — dash surfaces and suggests, nothing auto-sends, nothing re-tiers itself**; cap = 1 proactive touch/agent/local Mon–Sun week (fold or `cap_override`; operational never counts); cooling = monitoring only (T1 21 · T2 42 · T3 90 d from `lastMeaningfulContact`); **a call is never folded** (its outcome is the record — reached = two-way; unreached = attempt count / BAD #). Bands are never stored — they move with the ladder.

**Monthly obligations feature — issue #165 (SHIPPED v1.28.0 2026-07-09).** Balance-sheet insight (Jason uploaded a Balance Sheet, wanted loan/tax payments captured): P&L correctly EXCLUDES loan PRINCIPAL + owner draws (not expenses — they're balance-sheet/equity); only interest is a P&L expense (already shown); payroll taxes ARE already on the P&L. Balance sheet shows balances OWED, not payments made → can't auto-import. So: user-level recurring `obligations` (label/amount/active) → TRUE cash break-even = operating cost + obligations. Built: migration `025_obligations.sql` (RUN ON DEV pending Jason), `obligationServices.js` + `obligationRoutes.js` (`/obligations` CRUD, mounted in server.js), `getCashMetrics` in `expenses.ts` (2 tests), frontend `obligationsService.ts` / `types/obligation.ts` / `components/expenses/ObligationsCard` (list + add/edit/delete + Obligations/mo + True cash out + True break-even RPM) wired into ExpensesPage. 54 tests green, build green. NOT committed. v1 SIMPLIFICATION: obligations applied uniformly to ALL months (not effective-dated). Separately told Jason: cost/mile + break-even inflated because his LOADS/miles for those months aren't fully entered (not a calc bug — ~2,000 mi/mo implies missing loads). **REFINEMENTS 2026-07-09 (dev feedback):** (a) month-selector chips now CHRONOLOGICAL (ascending, oldest→newest — was reversed); (b) cost/mile + break-even + true break-even now **BLENDED over trailing 3 months** (Σcost ÷ Σmiles over the same window — variable cost moves with miles so both must be trailing; not this-month-cost ÷ avg-miles) labeled "· Nmo" (`ExpensesPage` `trailing` memo; obligations card gets avg-monthly cost+miles); (c) obligations card hint "loan **principal only** — interest already on P&L" (Jason caught the interest double-count: full payment double-counts the P&L interest). 54 tests green, build green. **SHIPPED v1.28.0** (PR #166, 2026-07-09) — obligations + all three refinements, backend+frontend+migration `025`. ⚠️ DEPLOY PENDING: migration `025_obligations.sql` must RUN ON PROD + deploy backend/frontend (Jason had only run it on dev). Design signed off via mockups: cost KPIs (monthly/weekly cost, cost-per-mile, break-even RPM, net margin), fixed/variable split, top categories (%rev), + **editable ledger** (reclassify fixed↔variable, edit value, add, delete; reclassify sets the category default going forward). Architecture: **FRONTEND parse (papaparse) → confirm-before-save → POST structured to backend**; backend stores monthly expense periods + classified lines + per-category fixed/variable defaults (NEW migration). **CHUNK 1 DONE + tested 2026-07-09:** `frontend/src/lib/pnl/parsePnl.ts` — `parsePnlRows` (collapses QuickBooks nested "Total for X" groups incl. parent-with-direct-amount; captures section totals; reconciles) + `classifyExpenses` (unchanged-months→fixed heuristic + trucking keyword defaults); **8 tests green** incl. single-month. **IMPORTANT (Jason 2026-07-09): the NORMAL upload is a SINGLE month (prior period only) — the 2-month comparison is a bonus, not the backbone.** So classification leans on REMEMBERED per-category defaults (backend) + keyword defaults; parser handles 1 or 2 amount columns (prior→null). Built against Jason's real P&L (fuel in COGS; Landstar commission netted into income vs app's gross load revenue — reconcile Phase 2). **ADDED to Expenses page scope (2026-07-09): YTD revenue-vs-expenses chart** — Jason has a year of P&Ls to upload; plot each stored month's P&L income vs total cost (gap = profit). Chunk 1 ✅ parser. **Chunk 2 ✅ backend (2026-07-09):** migration `024_expenses.sql` ran on dev (3 tables: expense_periods / expense_lines / expense_category_defaults + `expense_type` enum); `backend/src/services/expenseServices.js` (`saveExpensePeriod` = upsert period + replace lines + remember defaults, in a txn via `db.pool.connect()`; `getExpensePeriods`, `getExpensePeriod` w/ lines, `getCategoryDefaults`, `addExpenseLine`/`patchExpenseLine`/`deleteExpenseLine` each `recomputePeriodTotals`); `expenseRoutes.js` mounted at `/expenses` in server.js; node-check clean. API: POST `/expenses`, GET `/expenses`, GET `/expenses/:id`, GET `/expenses/defaults`, POST `/expenses/:id/lines`, PATCH/DELETE `/expenses/lines/:lineId`. **Chunk 3 ✅ (2026-07-09):** papaparse installed; `services/expensesService.ts`, `lib/pnl/parseFile.ts` (papaparse→parsePnlRows→classify w/ remembered defaults + `labelToMonth`), `lib/metrics/expenses.ts` (`getExpenseMetrics` — 4 tests), `types/expense.ts`; components under `components/expenses/`: `ExpenseUpload` (upload→confirm→save), `ExpenseLedger` (fixed/variable groups, reclassify/edit-value/add/delete), `ExpenseYtdChart` (recharts income-vs-cost); `ExpensesPage` (KPIs, split, ledger, chart, month selector); route `/expenses` + nav. Month miles from loads' odometers. **52 tests green, build green. DEV-TESTED 2026-07-09 — Jason uploaded real May+June P&L: PARSER WORKED (all categories/amounts correct, reconciles). Bugs found + FIXED same day:** (1) used dead Tabler `ti` icon classes (app = lucide-react, no Tabler font) → all Expenses action icons + shipped Lanes chevrons INVISIBLE → swapped ALL to lucide (ExpenseLedger, ExpenseUpload, LanesTable; AlertBanners still uses `ti` but never rendered yet → fix at alert-engine); (2) YTD chart → **2 clean lines (Income/Cost) + rich hover tooltip (income/cost/profit/margin%)**, moved under KPIs; (3) add-expense cancelable; (4) COGS "Cost of goods sold" → **"Fuel"** on import (`parseFile`; NEW uploads only — existing saved rows unchanged until re-uploaded). Minor (not bugs): high CPM/break-even = thin dev-load miles (real data fixes); $0/negative lines are real P&L data (deletable in ledger).** Ship = backend+frontend+migration `024`, `feat` → **1.27.0**. Then Phase 2 = wire CPM app-wide (real break-even replaces $2.96, net profit on dashboard, profit-per-lane). **Phase 2 (separate arc):** wire CPM app-wide — real break-even replaces hardcoded $2.96, net-profit on dashboard, profit-per-lane on Lanes. **OBLIGATIONS FOLD-IN (SHIPPED v1.29.0, PR #167, 2026-07-09 — dev-verified by Jason "works perfectly"; frontend-only, NO migration):** Jason's feedback post-deploy — obligations only moved the one card, headline KPIs + chart ignored them; he wants ONE set of numbers (true cost = P&L operating + active obligations; revenue − true cost = margin; per-mile off true cost). Built: `getTrueMonthly(operating, oblig, income)` → {trueMonthlyCost, trueWeeklyCost, trueNetMargin} (this-month basis) + 2 tests; ExpensesPage lifts obligations to page level (`reloadObligations`), computes `obligationsTotal` (active only), feeds KPIs via getTrueMonthly (dollars, this-month) + getCashMetrics (per-mile, trailing blend); Monthly/Weekly/Cost-mile/Break-even/Net-margin ALL now true-cost; chart gets `obligationsTotal` prop (adds to each month's cost → "true cost" line); ObligationsCard slimmed to a pure LIST manager (removed the redundant true-cash stat trio — now the headline) + added a real **active toggle** (Eye/EyeOff → patch active; the old card had NO toggle despite the model supporting it); reconciliation line on fixed/var card (operating + obligations = true monthly). obligationsTotal=0 → identical to operating (no regression). 56 tests + build green. Semver: feat → **1.29.0** (frontend-only, NO migration). **OPS SHEET RECON (2026-07-09):** Google Drive `DTS_Operations` sheet (id `1Lz6fEmB-mNZzsqRcm7xfpu4Yh4Prh88QvU8JrXXMjRg`, in My Drive > Operations > Workbooks) — the "Expenses" tab is the SOURCE of the hardcoded $2.96 and a hand-built version of the app's Expenses page. **Revenue targets** = tiers on break-even/LOADED mile: Walk-Away = total CPM ($2.99) ÷ PTR (0.73) = $4.10; Minimum(15%)=×1.15=$4.71; Target(35%)=×1.35=$5.53; Strong(60%)=×1.60=$6.55. Assumptions on sheet: Avg miles 8500/mo, PTR 0.73 (loaded÷total). Also Breakeven Gross $8,096/wk (= weekly cost ÷ PTR) + Daily Rate $1,582 (gross-pace targets). PROPOSAL PENDING: derive these live from the app's true break-even RPM (already computed) as a "Rate targets" strip on Expenses + echo Target on Dashboard; tiers 15/35/60 configurable. **DECISION (Jason 2026-07-09): live-from-loads, BUT the break-even basis = avg cost ÷ avg loaded miles over the 3 most recent COMPLETE months (EXCLUDE the in-progress current calendar month — it inflates per-mile since miles lag cost). Anchored to today, NOT tied to the selected month tag (targets are forward pricing guidance).** Same window for numerator + denominator. Build order: ship fold-in v1.29.0 first, then Rate Targets strip (Expenses) + Target rate on Dashboard as its own issue. Open sub-Q at build time: keep Expenses per-mile KPIs trailing-from-selected (historical context) vs unify to the anchored prior-3-complete basis. This is the natural Phase 2. **DASHBOARD PLACEMENT DECIDED (Jason 2026-07-09, via mockup):** dedicated "Rate and pace targets" card DIRECTLY UNDER the KPI strip (above the charts). Contents: (a) **rate ladder** per loaded mile — walk-away/target/strong with a marker for current Avg RPM, colored red-below-breakeven→green-above-target; (b) **this-week gross pace bar** — MUST include BOOKED freight (sum rate of all loads running the current week Mon–Sun regardless of status: booked/in-transit/delivered, anchored on delivery date — mid-week committed+earned view), with TWO ticks = break-even floor + 35% target ("show both", my rec, Jason deferred to me); (c) **daily rate target**. Same rate-ladder strip also on Expenses page by the break-even. **This work RETIRES `BREAK_EVEN_RPM=2.96` (frontend/src/lib/constants/targets.ts, has a TODO) AND fixes a real bug: dashboard currently compares Avg RPM (revenue/LOADED mile) to $2.96 (cost/TOTAL mile) = apples-to-oranges, flatters profitability; correct comparison is vs walk-away (~$4.10, break-even per LOADED mile).** **MATH CATCH:** Jason's sheet Weekly Gross $8,096 + Daily $1,582 = cost ÷ PTR (÷0.73 an EXTRA time — assumes earning the rate on empty miles too); correct break-even gross = the cost itself (weekly/daily TRUE cost incl. obligations, ~$6,800/wk). Derive live + correctly, do NOT copy the sheet. Daily = monthly true cost ÷ 22 working days (configurable), weekly = monthly ÷ 4.333. Tiers 15/35/60% + working-days = configurable constants to start. PTR live-derived = loaded÷total over prior-3-complete-months. Its own issue/PR → v1.30.0. **SHIPPED v1.30.0 (PR #168, 2026-07-09 — dev-verified "looks great"; frontend-only NO migration):** engine `frontend/src/lib/metrics/rateTargets.ts` (pure, `now` param for testability): `completeMonthsBefore` (3 complete months before current, excludes in-progress), `getCostBasis` (blends true cost=operating+active-obligations ÷ loaded miles over those months, only months WITH a P&L counted so cost/miles stay aligned; returns breakEvenRpm=walkAway, windowRpm=your rate, trueMonthlyCost, months), `getRateLadder` (walkAway/minimum/target/strong = ×1+tier), `getGrossTargets` (weekly=÷4.333, daily=÷22, +target markup), `payWeekRange` (Wed–Tue via `PAY_WEEK_START_DOW=3`), `getWeekBookedGross` (Σ rate of non-cancelled loads delivering in [start,end), all statuses). Constants in `lib/constants/targets.ts`: `RATE_TIERS{minimum:.15,target:.35,strong:.6}`, `WORKING_DAYS_PER_MONTH=22`, `PAY_WEEK_START_DOW=3` (TODO→settings page), `BREAK_EVEN_RPM=2.96` now FALLBACK-only. Hook `hooks/useRateTargets.ts` (fetches periods+obligations, assembles). Components `components/dashboard/RateLadder.tsx` (shared ladder viz, marker) + `RateTargetsCard.tsx` (ladder + this-week booked PaceBar w/ floor+target ticks + daily target). DashboardPage: card under KPI strip; **retired hardcoded $2.96 → Avg RPM card status + RpmChart now use liveBreakEven=walkAway (fallback 2.96); FIXED apples-to-oranges (was Avg RPM vs cost/total-mile).** Dashboard ladder marker = all-time avgRpm (matches Avg RPM KPI, self-consistent); Expenses ladder strip marker = windowRpm (recent). 12 new tests (68 total), build green. Rate ladder also on ExpensesPage (anchored today, not selected month). Caveat: per-mile numbers only as good as ENTERED LOADS; break-even reads high until miles in. **REFINEMENT (SHIPPED v1.30.1, PR #169, 2026-07-09):** dashboard "your rate" split into two: **Avg RPM KPI = rolling 3-COMPLETE-month blended RPM** (`getWindowRpm`, loads-only, relabeled "Avg RPM · 3mo"; replaced all-time `getAverageRPM`) — same window as the break-even it's compared to; **card ladder marker = CURRENT pay-week RPM** (`getWeekRpm` = this-week booked+delivered revenue ÷ loaded miles, captioned "marker = this week"). Both added as pure fns + 4 tests (72 total). Expenses ladder marker stays windowRpm (3mo). Hook exposes weekRpm + rollingRpm.

Now auto-links.** `unit` (tractor/trailer) stays = asset-TYPE choice, not entity selection. Jason HASN'T loaded starter schedule on prod yet (waited for fleet backfill) — after this ships + Railway backend redeploys, loading it will auto-link to his prod truck (3686d2e6…)/trailer (7c2988b1…). REUSE this pattern for LOADS truck/driver/trailer assignment later. Frontend maintenance page needs NO change for single-rig. **SERVICE BOTH-UNITS SHIPPED v1.35.0 (PR #176, 2026-07-09; migration 029 applied dev + PROD):** a shop visit/invoice often covers tractor+trailer together (combined Landstar Ultimate PM, one cost) but a service could only be logged against one unit. Migration `029_service_both.sql`: `maintenance_services` +`trailer_hub INTEGER`, `unit` CHECK now allows `both`, and existing `unit='trailer'` rows moved their reading `odometer`→`trailer_hub` (dev had 2 such rows: 445000/456123; prod had 0 services). **Semantics: `odometer`=TRUCK reading, `trailer_hub`=TRAILER reading** (was: single `odometer` held whichever unit's reading). Backend `maintenanceServices.js`: `isServiceUnit`=tractor|trailer|both; `linkFor` sets truck_id when tractor|both, trailer_id when trailer|both; `createMaintenanceService` inserts both readings + on item-completion resets EACH item against the reading for ITS OWN unit (item.unit==='trailer'→trailer_hub else odometer, via `RETURNING item_id, unit`); getMaintenanceServices/patch/RETURNING +trailer_hub. Frontend: `ServiceUnit` type (+`both`), ServiceForm has "Both (truck + trailer)" option → reveals TWO reading fields (Truck odometer + Trailer hub) + completable-items list spans both units; ServicesTab odo column renders truck/trailer/both(stacked) via `reading()`; Truck/Trailer detail latest-reading + `useMaintenanceAlerts`/MaintenancePage per-unit max now include `both` services and read the right column (truck→odometer, trailer→trailer_hub). **Deploy order (done): migration 029 + backend redeploy BEFORE frontend** so old backend never receives `both`/`trailer_hub`. (Login-kick Jason hit = 401 = expired session, not a bug; app hard-logs-out on any 401 — soften later w/ longer token/refresh.) **HOTFIX v1.35.1 (PR #177, 2026-07-09, backend-only):** the item-reset loop used `INSERT INTO maintenance_service_items (...) SELECT ... RETURNING item_id, unit` but RETURNING only sees the TARGET table and the join table is just `(service_id, item_id)` → every service completing an item 500'd with `column "unit" does not exist`. Fix: `SELECT unit FROM maintenance_items` first (also the ownership check), then plain `INSERT ... VALUES`, then per-unit reset. **LESSON: build + 90 unit tests are GREEN but exercise ZERO real SQL — a RETURNING-on-wrong-table bug sailed through my "verified" claim. For DB query changes, actually run the statement shapes against dev (e.g. a `DO $$ ... RAISE EXCEPTION 'ROLLBACK_OK' $$` that rolls back) before claiming verified.**

**Dashboard redesign (issue #161) — SHIPPED v1.26.0.** Bento layout + top-5 agents (clickable → agent detail) + Loads-MTD KPI + What's-next (booked/in-transit) + **reserved alert-banner region** (empty `Alert[]` source now; renders nothing when empty). Metrics `getLoadsMonthly`/`getTopAgentsByRevenue`/`getUpcomingLoads` (frozen-clock tested, 37 total green); components `AlertBanners`/`TopAgents`/`WhatsNext` under `components/dashboard/`; `DashboardPage` rewritten to bento. Instructor kept Revenue YTD (→ 5 KPIs) + the RPM chart (mockup omitted both — did NOT silently drop existing features; Jason can veto). **REFINEMENTS folded in 2026-07-09 per Jason's dev feedback:** top agents now = revenue over last 90 days + ≥2-load floor + shows load count (window kills stale, floor kills one-offs — same pattern as Lanes); drill-downs everywhere (agents→detail; outstanding/what's-next/recent-loads → `/loads/:id`; added `load_id` to those metric shapes); new **Recent loads** tile (`getRecentDeliveredLoads`); Outstanding shows total + avg aging (`getOutstandingSummary` — NOTE: aging of unpaid, not true DSO; real days-to-pay needs a payment-date we don't track yet). 40 tests green, strict build green. **BUG FIXED 2026-07-09:** getLoads LIST query omitted `agent_id` → `load.agent_id` undefined on dashboard → agent links went to `/agents/undefined` AND top-agents grouped every load under one undefined key (list was WRONG, not just mislinked). Fix: added `loads.agent_id` to getLoads SELECT (`backend/src/services/loadServices.js`). **This makes the #161 ship BACKEND + frontend (still no DB migration) — restart dev backend to re-test.** Latent mismatch flagged: getLoads also omits broker_id/agent_email/market ids the `Load` type declares — cleanup later. Not committed. Ship = `feat` → **1.26.0**.

**FUTURE FEATURE (captured 2026-07-09): "times a top agent" badge** on agent cards/directory (e.g. "3× top agent") so agents who fell off the current top-5 (via the 90d window / ≥2 floor) still show their track record = good partners. Recommended impl: compute from load history — per calendar month, that month's top-5 by revenue, tally per agent — **NO new table/cron**. Its own feature, AFTER the dashboard; pairs with the "richer agents list" backlog item.

**ROADMAP (agreed 2026-07-08, in order):** (1) THIS dashboard redesign → (2) **monthly P&L upload → cost → net profit**: Jason uploads P&L once/month, app parses (format matters: CSV/Excel >> PDF) and shows parsed figures to CONFIRM before saving (escapes his error-prone ops sheet); total cost from P&L, miles from loads → CPM computed; replaces hardcoded `BREAK_EVEN_RPM=2.96`; unlocks net-profit tiles. **Google-Sheets live-sync REJECTED** (error-prone, fragile per-load). → (3) **alert engine** (maintenance from truck mileage/service intervals, compliance from doc-expiry dates) → feeds the reserved banner region; needs Jason to start tracking service intervals + doc expiry dates. **EXPANDED 2026-07-09 (Jason):** also surface **intelligence / recommendation banners** — RULE-BASED (deterministic) anomaly detection + operational recs from the app's own data (expense category vs trailing avg, deadhead spike, below-break-even lane, aging AR). Same `Alert` type + banner region (add `kind: 'insight'`). **Rules-FIRST, NOT LLM** (hallucination risk on financial advice; rules are cheap, explainable, testable — instructor pushed back on leading with AI; LLM narrative synthesis a possible later layer). Needs a HISTORY BASELINE to define "unusually high" → do AFTER P&L gives the expense history. Guard against alert fatigue (few high-signal rules).

Dev-tested by Jason (passed). **SHIPPED 2026-07-08:** PR #160 squash-merged (`950cb4f`), tagged **v1.25.0**, frontend-only (no migration) → Vercel auto-deploy. ### dash — prior version 1.46.0 (compliance hub 2026-07-10)

**COMPLIANCE HUB — SHIPPED v1.46.0 (PR #191, closes #190, 2026-07-10; backend + frontend, migration 030).** Single Compliance page tracking renewal/expiry-dated docs across 3 scopes: **business / driver / vehicle**. Design = maintenance's twin, date-keyed instead of miles; alerts flow into the dashboard region already wired for `kind:"compliance"`. **Data model:** `compliance_items` table (030, user-scoped, optional driver/truck/trailer link): scope, label, category, issued_on, expires_on, renewal_months, warn_lead_days, doc_number, notes, active. **CDL DECISION (Jason inverted my first call):** CDL stays STORED on the driver record (number/state/expiration/endorsements = identity, won't fit generic table), but the compliance hub is the SOLE EDIT SURFACE (writes back via patchDriver) and the driver page is READ-ONLY for CDL — removed CDL from `DRIVER_FIELDS`, added "managed on Compliance page" link on DriverDetailPage. One source of truth. **Engine** `lib/metrics/compliance.ts` (10 tests): `computeComplianceDue` (valid/expiring/expired/unknown; `daysUntil` = whole-day UTC math, no shift), `complianceSummary` (counts + status **cleared/grounded** — grounded ONLY when a driver/vehicle doc expired; expired BUSINESS doc counts but doesn't ground), `complianceAlerts` (expired=critical before expiring=warning), `itemToCheckable`/`cdlToCheckable` (CDL_WARN_LEAD_DAYS=45). **Page** `CompliancePage.tsx`: green CLEARED TO ROLL / red GROUNDED `<Stamp>`, valid/expiring/expired KPIs, per-entity sections (Business, Driver·name, Vehicle·unit, Trailer·unit) with drill-down links + inline add/edit/delete via `ComplianceItemForm` (common-doc PRESETS per scope so setup = dates not typing) + inline CDL editor. Backend: `complianceServices.js` (CRUD, per-scope entity ownership via reconcileScope), `complianceRoutes.js` (`/compliance`, national-diesel-style handle()), `complianceValidation.js`, mounted in server.js. Sidebar: **Compliance** top-level (Dashboard·Freight·Fleet·Compliance·Expenses). Dashboard merges compliance+maintenance alerts. **⚠️ OPS GOTCHA (learned this build): backend/.env DATABASE_URL points to PROD, not dev** — so NEVER run mutating CRUD through the backend pool (writes prod); verify query changes by running the SQL against the DEV Supabase project via MCP execute_sql. Migration 030 applied to BOTH dev + prod (idempotent CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS). Dev seeded w/ demo items (medical/2290/registration/LLC) + Jason's dev driver given a CDL. Strict build clean, 143 tests. **METRIC FIXES v1.56.1 (PR #207, 2026-07-11) — two corrections Jason caught before Compliance:** (1) **Weekly target/pace was counting BOOKED + in-transit freight as "hit target"** — booking loads ahead flashed the win before anything was hauled (and would evaporate on TONU/cancel). Split the week into EARNED (delivered) vs COMMITTED (booked/in-transit): added `getWeekEarnedGross` (delivered-only) to rateTargets.ts; `useRateTargets` now exposes `weekEarned` + `weekBooked`; RateTargetsCard shows "This week · earned" = weekEarned, TargetBurst + beatTarget fire on EARNED only, PaceBar renders committed as a faded 30%-opacity extension behind the solid earned fill + a "+ $X committed, still to haul" line; **grind meter grades EVERY week on earned** (computeGrind uses getWeekEarnedGross — past weeks identical since delivered==booked historically in his data, in-progress week now honest; GrindMeter "home" copy → "Nothing delivered yet this week."). NOTE mid-week earned is often $0 until loads deliver — intended/honest, not a bug. Root cause was NOT a date leak: `loadsInWeek` correctly windows on delivery_date, so a load dated 3 weeks out never hit this week; the issue was un-hauled freight counting. (2) **Season recap headline Miles was loaded-only** (dropped deadhead) → `RecapStats.totalMiles` = Σ per delivered load of (odometer_end − odometer_start), **fallback to loaded_miles when a load lacks odometer readings** (so total never reads < loaded); kept `loadedMiles` for avgRpm (= gross ÷ loaded, revenue math, unchanged); RecapPoster MILES hero → totalMiles. Verified vs prod: his load odometers tile continuously so Σ deltas = true driven miles incl deadhead (e.g. a 552-loaded run = 815 odo = 263 deadhead). **FUEL MPG VERIFIED for Jason (2026-07-11 fill, on request):** app ~6.1 is CORRECT — last full tank Jul 8 @ odo 582,450 → today Jul 11 @ 583,244 = 794 mi ÷ 130.067 gal = **6.10 MPG**; his truck dash's 7.0 is ~15% factory-optimism (estimates off injector duty, reads high — trust the app). `fuelEconomy.mpgWindows` = full-to-full miles ÷ all gallons since last full (partials roll in); FULL_THRESHOLD=120 gal. 171 tests, build green, frontend-only.

**SETTLEMENT SCHEDULE → NET REVENUE — PHASE 1 SHIPPED v1.57.0 (PR #208, 2026-07-12).** Jason (Landstar BCO) enters each load's FULL customer rate but keeps only **65% linehaul + 8% flatbed trailer = 73%, 100% fuel surcharge** (from his signed ICOA — see auto-memory [[landstar-settlement-schedule]]). App was reporting pre-cut gross as "revenue" — ran 14–27% high vs his actual P&L income. **FIX:** per-user `settlement_schedules` table (migration `033`: linehaul_pct/trailer_pct/fuel_surcharge_pct/accessorial_pct, defaults 1/0/1/1 so net==gross when unconfigured [productizes; own-authority=100%], CHECK 0–2); `settlementScheduleServices.js` (get returns DEFAULTS if no row; upsert partial-merges) + `settlementScheduleRoutes.js` GET/PUT mounted `/settlement-schedule`; **`getLoads`/`getLoad` now return `gross_revenue` + `net_revenue`** computed server-side via `LEFT JOIN settlement_schedules` + `ROUND(linehaul*(lh_pct+tr_pct) + fuel_surcharge*fsc_pct + accessorials*acc_pct, 2)`. Frontend: `Load` type +`gross_revenue?`/`net_revenue?` (OPTIONAL — metrics fall back to linehaul+fsc+accessorials sum when absent, so fixtures/local loads don't break); `rateTargets.loadRevenue` now returns NET (`Number(net_revenue)` else gross via `Number.isFinite`), NEW `loadGross` returns full rate; `dashboard.getLoadRevenue` routes through loadRevenue; recap/pace/grind/RPM all net automatically. **TARGETS SELF-CORRECTED** — pace/grind now compare NET take vs cost, so "hit target" = netting cost+margin (were ~27% too low, counting full pre-cut gross). Relabeled dashboard "Net revenue · MTD/YTD", "Net RPM · 3mo", "Net rate per loaded mile". NEW **Settings page** (`/settings`, sidebar footer link) edits the schedule w/ live "$2,000+$300 FSC → $X net" preview; `services/settlementScheduleService.ts` (coerces to numbers) + `types/settlementSchedule.ts`. **VERIFIED vs his real books: schedule-net within 0.9% of his P&L income over Q2** ($78,651 vs $79,347; monthly ±8% = settlement-vs-delivery-date timing, washes out); in-transit load $6,703.80 gross → **$5,124.30 net** confirmed on prod. **Migration 033 + his prod schedule (65/8/100/100) applied to PROD BEFORE merge** (getLoads LEFT JOINs the table — would 500 if absent). `accessorial_type` is FREE-TEXT (LoadDetailPage capitalizes user input) so per-type %s DEFERRED — single `accessorial_pct` for now (his accessorials ~$0). Cost basis confirmed ALREADY rolling-3-month (`getCostBasis` `completeMonthsBefore(now,3)`); Jason chose to LEAVE it at 3-month (June maintenance spike $4,990 vs ~$1,200 norm lifts blended target ~$380/wk, accepted as real cost). Followed new [[ask-before-shipping]] rule (built → dev-tested by Jason → shipped on his go). 173 tests, build green. **PHASE 2 PENDING (agreed): rate ladder "show BOTH" — net rate/mile (truth) + the full booked rate needed to clear each tier (pricing gross-up ÷ effective take), using `loadGross`.** Also deferred: per-type accessorial rates + a controlled accessorial dropdown; widen/amortize cost window if maintenance lumpiness distorts.

**RATE LADDER = GROSS PER *TOTAL* MILE (Jason's booking basis) — SHIPPED v1.58.1 (PR #210, 2026-07-12). CRITICAL — do NOT relitigate:** Jason books loads in **gross $/mile DRIVEN**, folding his deadhead into the miles himself (200mi to pickup = 200 unpaid miles he adds to the denominator). His break-even floor = **rolling cost per TOTAL mile ($3.17) ÷ his 0.73 linehaul keep = $4.34 gross/mile**, scaled by 15/35/60% tiers. Anything he books above $4.34 (with deadhead in his miles) = profit; it's CONSERVATIVE because he keeps 100% of FSC+accessorials on top of the 73%. **The ladder now shows: walk-away = costPerTotalMile ÷ linehaulTake ($4.34), marker = grossPerTotalMile (his actual gross rate/mile, $4.52 = gross $101,152 ÷ 22,368 total mi).** `getCostBasis` gained `costPerTotalMile` + `grossPerTotalMile`; `useRateTargets` builds `bookingLadder` + `grossRate`; RateLadder reverted to simple (dropped the v1.58.0 per-rung "book $X" annotation — plotting a NET marker next to GROSS rungs caused a multi-turn blowup); Expenses ladder matched. **THE TWO SEPARATE ~30% HAIRCUTS (root of all the confusion, both ~0.7 for him so easy to conflate):** (1) DEADHEAD — his loaded/total = 0.67 (33% empty); turns cost/total ($3.17) → cost/loaded ($4.72). (2) LANDSTAR CUT — he keeps 73% of linehaul; turns net rate → gross booking rate. They STACK and apply at different steps. $4.34/total-mile ≡ $6.47/loaded-mile (same break-even, different denominator); Jason books per TOTAL mile so $4.34 is his number. I burned ~5 turns wrongly insisting on loaded-mile framing + comparing his NET rate ($5.25/loaded) to a GROSS booked number ($6.47) — apples-to-oranges; he was right, I was overcomplicating. Freight bill example (BCO Load Detail): linehaul $8,955 + FSC $1,110.42 ($0.62/mi×1,791) + Tarp $150 = $10,215.42 customer gross; his cut = 73%×linehaul + 100%×FSC + tarp% → net ~$7,797. 176 tests. **NEXT: accessorial per-type %s (Jason: NOT all 100% — ICOA Appendix A-1: tarp/detention/toll/etc 100%, surveillance/security 85%, unlisted 65%; his `accessorial_pct=1.0` in settlement_schedules overstates non-100% ones), then Phase 3 (net-revenue coverage sweep: agent/lane/truck/driver pages still gross).**

1. **ACCESSORIAL PER-TYPE %s — in progress, BLOCKED on Jason's input.** Recon done: his prod accessorials have FREE-TEXT spelling variants (Tarp×20 vs "Tarp Charges"×1; Hazmat/"Hazmat "/Hazmt/"Hazardous Cargo Charge"; "Stop Off"/Stop) + 3 types the ICOA doesn't pin down. **Plan:** per-type pay-% (Settings-managed, seeded from ICOA), applied in the getLoads net computation (replace flat `accessorial_pct`); make accessorial entry a DROPDOWN (clean list, no typos) + consolidate his existing variants. Seed CLEAR ones from ICOA: Tarp/Detention/Tolls/Loading = 100%. **SHIPPED v1.58.3 (PR #212, 2026-07-13).** **VERIFIED the rates EMPIRICALLY against Jason's actual Landstar settlements** (his instinct: verify, don't guess — and 2 of his 3 guesses were WRONG). **METHOD (reusable):** settlements live in Drive `Bookkeeping/[year]/[month]/[MM-DD-YYYY]_Settlement`; freight bills in `Operations/Loads/[freightbill#]` where freightbill# = loads.load_number. Find the settlement via `fullText contains '<load_number>'`. The settlement pays each line as TWO rows — **tractor 65% + trailer 8% = 73%** — for linehaul AND base-rate accessorials; FSC/tarp show one 100% row. **VERIFIED RATES:** Hazmat = **73%** (NOT his guess 100% — settlement 05-27 load 6723509: $300 → $195 tractor + $24 trailer = $219); Stop-Off = **73%** (NOT his guess 65% — same load: $50 → $32.50 + $4 = $36.50; he forgot the +8% trailer); **EVC = 0%** (CONFIRMED — settlement 04-29 load 7413468 has NO EVC line; the $582.80 freight-bill EVC is kept entirely by Landstar). Tarp/FSC/Detention/Tolls/Loading = **100%**. **KEY INSIGHT: the trailer 8% rides on base-rate accessorials just like linehaul, so the honest UNLISTED DEFAULT = 73% (= linehaulTake), NOT 65%.** IMPLEMENTATION: migration `034_accessorial_pay_rates.sql` (per-user per-type `accessorial_pay_rates` table, PK user_id+accessorial_type, pay_pct 0–2); `getLoads`/`getLoad` net_revenue LEFT JOINs it (`a.amount * COALESCE(apr.pay_pct, COALESCE(ss.accessorial_pct,1))`), fallback to `settlement_schedules.accessorial_pct` (set to **0.73** for his user). Seeded dev + PROD with his 12 free-text variant names (Tarp/"Tarp Charges"/Detention/Tolls/Loading=1.0; Hazmat/"Hazmat "/Hazmt/"Hazardous Cargo Charge"/"Stop Off"/Stop=0.73; "High Value-EVC"=0.0). Prod table+seed applied BEFORE merge (getLoads LEFT JOINs it). Verified prod: 6723509→$4,041.12, 7413468→$4,138.15. **ACCESSORIAL EDITOR + DROPDOWN SHIPPED v1.59.0 (PR #213, 2026-07-13):** `/accessorial-rates` CRUD (`accessorialRateServices.js` get/upsert/delete + `accessorialRateRoutes.js` GET/PUT/DELETE :type, mounted); frontend `services/accessorialRateService.ts` + `types/accessorialRate.ts` + `components/settings/AccessorialRatesCard.tsx` (list/add/edit-%/delete, added to SettingsPage below the settlement schedule) + LoadDetailPage accessorial type now a `<select>` off the rate list with "Other…" free-text escape (falls to schedule's `accessorial_pct` default 0.73). **CONSOLIDATED his 12 free-text spelling variants → canonical 7 on dev + PROD (net-neutral rename, verified test loads unchanged $4,041.12/$4,138.15):** Tarp/Detention/Tolls/Loading=100%, Hazmat/Stop-Off=73%, High Value-EVC=0%. `accessorials` data renamed to canonical (Tarp Charges→Tarp; Hazmat /Hazmt/Hazardous Cargo Charge→Hazmat; Stop Off/Stop→Stop-Off) BEFORE merge. STILL DEFERRED: Settings UI already edits the settlement schedule (SettingsPage) — that's done; per-diem/other settings later. **METRIC-TRANSPARENCY GUIDE SHIPPED v1.60.0 (PR #214, 2026-07-13):** GuidePage (`/guide`) rewritten — leads with "The money": 8 metric explainers (net revenue, true cost/mile, break-even/rate-to-book, rate ladder, weekly/daily targets, RPM, deadhead, MPG), each = plain-English + Formula box + worked example + Why, with inline SVG/CSS visuals on the key ones (net-revenue keep-% bars, cost→÷keep→book-rate ChainBox, MiniLadder). **EXPENSES PAGE CLEANUP SHIPPED v1.61.1 (PR #216, 2026-07-13):** relabeled the ambiguous per-mile KPIs — "Cost / mile"→"Cost / total mile" (trueCpm = cost÷total miles, $3.17), "Break-even RPM"→"Break-even / loaded mi" (trueBreakEvenRpm = cost÷loaded miles, net, $4.72); added a clarifier (cost over all miles vs break-even over paid miles, gap = deadhead) + a live cost→÷keep→book chain box above the booking ladder ($3.17/total mi → ÷73% keep → $4.34 to book, using rateBasis.costPerTotalMile/linehaulTake/rateLadder.walkAway). Defuses the multi-turn three-numbers ($3.17/$4.72/$4.34) confusion on the page itself. Frontend-only, 176 tests.

**PHASE 3 NET-REVENUE SWEEP SHIPPED v1.62.0 (PR #217, 2026-07-13):** the final decision was NUANCED, not "net everywhere" — Jason's rule: **money the company KEEPS = net; market economics + the pricing lens = gross.** Net now: Truck/Driver "Net revenue · all time" (recent-load amounts too), Trailer NEW "Net revenue · all time" + "Loads hauled" stat ("what's the trailer made me"), LoadDetail NEW "Your net" line under the rate breakdown (only shows when net≠gross), LoadsPage "This month · net" + "Outstanding AR". **Kept GROSS on purpose (Jason's explicit call): AGENT + LANE pages** ("the agent/lane's economics is the full market rate, independent of my cut"), the LoadsPage **"Gross" column** + LoadDetail headline (the deal size / booked rate), **every rate-per-mile** (rate card, load detail, loads-page avg — the pricing lens), and **Pipeline = a COUNT** (Jason corrected me — it's booked-load count, no $). loads.ts loadsKpis now imports rateTargets' net `loadRevenue` as `loadNet` for the dollar KPIs (deliveredGross→deliveredNet field rename) but keeps a gross sum for rpm; killed the duplicate gross helper concern. New test pins net-dollars/gross-rpm split (177 tests). getGrossRevenue/getAverageRPM on agent.ts + lanes.ts left gross (correct).

**PER-ASSET ATTRIBUTION FIX v1.62.1 (PR #218, 2026-07-13) — the v1.62.0 truck/driver/trailer "net revenue · all time" was DOUBLY WRONG; Jason caught it because the numbers didn't tie to his dashboard:** (1) it summed net over ALL loads regardless of status — including 7 CANCELLED ($18,476, never happened) + booked/in-transit ($7,230, unearned); (2) it credited the trailer with the FULL load net instead of its slice. **THE CONFIRMED MODEL (asset-dependent — each page sums only loads tied to its truck_id/driver_id/trailer_id, so multi-truck fleets attribute per asset): TRUCK & DRIVER = FULL net of their DELIVERED+PAID loads** (they're on every load → both tie to dashboard "Net revenue · YTD" = $141,355.99, which is delivered+2026 only via getRevenueYTD). **TRAILER = its OWN share only = trailer_pct(8%)×linehaul + trailer_pct×base-rate accessorials** (the ones paid at linehaul_pct+trailer_pct=0.73: hazmat/stop-off/unlisted); FSC + flat-100% accessorials (tarp/detention/tolls/loading) stay with the TRACTOR. **BASIS = EARNED = delivered AND paid** (Jason's call; all 47 delivered happen to be paid so = $141,356). Backend added per-load `trailer_net` computed column to getLoads+getLoad (no migration, no stored column); frontend `loadTrailerNet` helper + pages filter `load_status==='delivered' && payment_status==='paid'`. **DATA FIX applied to prod: trailer 780991 (in_service 2026-02-03) was assigned to 3 January loads from before Jason owned it → set trailer_id=NULL on them (delivery_date<'2026-02-03'), dropping trailer $12,522→$11,066 (44 loads).** Truck/driver keep loadRevenue (full net); trailer uses loadTrailerNet. Driver page = full net is CORRECT (driver generated the whole load; not part of equipment split). Also still deferred: per-diem tracker, truck-payoff tracker, cash-flow forecast, load scorer, deadhead radar, RLS on the newer tables.**

**RECAP PRESTIGE TIERS + CEREMONY POPS SHIPPED v1.63.0 (PR #219, 2026-07-13):** Jason's asks — recap must look BACKWARD (never the in-progress period), fire a ceremonial pop when a period closes, and both the pop AND the card scale in prestige AND data with the period (month < quarter < year; year keeps his truck avatar). **BUG FIXED:** `resolvePeriod` year used `y-ago` (current, unfinished year) while month/quarter used `-1`; now `y-1-ago`. `latestRecapWithData(loads,now)` (year→quarter→month, longest with data) lands the recap page + dashboard link on the newest FINISHED period (Q2 2026 for him; 2025 empty). **PRESTIGE = bronze(month)/silver(quarter)/gold(year)** via shared `lib/constants/recapTiers.ts` (RECAP_TIERS — one source of truth for card + pop). **DATA SCALES:** month=core 6 stats; quarter=+detail tiles+net+3-bar strip+top lane/agent; year=full poster+12-month strip+truck-avatar banner. New `monthlyGross` series in computeRecap (1/3/12 bars). **CEREMONY:** new `components/comic/RecapCeremony.tsx` screen-takeover, fires once per period-close via the existing per-device "seen" store; added as a new `"recap"` AwardTier in awards.ts (earnedAwards emits `recap:{scope}:{label}` for each completed period w/ data; newAwards plays them month→quarter→year crescendo BEFORE marquees; AwardPopHost renders recap takeover above marquee). `useAwardPops` now also returns truckAvatarUrl (year medallion + banner). Ceremony CTA navigates to /recap w/ router state `{scope}`; RecapPage honors nav scope else auto-scopes to latest-with-data. Preview via `?awarddemo` (DEMO_AWARDS has all 3 recap tiers). Design approved via 2 show_widget mockups first. Frontend-only, no migration, 179 tests. NOTE: blank-page scare during dev was a stale HMR bundle, not a real bug (no import cycle). **SUBSUMPTION FIX v1.63.1 (PR #220, 2026-07-13):** on prod the June pop fired and masked Q2. Rule now — a smaller period that CLOSES ON THE SAME DAY as a bigger one is subsumed: only the grandest ceremony pops (June ends when Q2 ends → Q2 pops, June suppressed; Dec hands off to Q4 + year). earnedAwards compares `resolvePeriod(scope,0,now).end` across month/quarter/year (sameClose → skip the smaller); newAwards now orders recaps GRANDEST-first (year→quarter→month, was crescendo month-first). Subsumed period still browsable on the recap page. Caveat: on a device that already fired June, Q2 got marked seen at the same instant so it won't re-pop there (fix corrects future boundaries only; `?awarddemo` previews the look). 182 tests. STILL QUEUED (separate): trophy-earn (Trophy Hall) → fire a ceremony/marquee pop; per-diem, truck-payoff, cash-flow forecast, RLS.**

**AWARD TIERS = THREE HOMES (DRIVER PAGE REWORK v1.64.0, PR #221, 2026-07-13) — Jason's model, now the durable structure:** the `earnedAwards` tiers map 1:1 to display homes so a POP always has a matching persistent home under the SAME name/icon. **`burst` → BADGES** (frequent wins; new badge area on the player card, record NUMBERS folded in as the badge detail — e.g. "Tight Lines · deadhead down to 7.2%", "Feather Foot · 6.9 mpg"; replaced the old "Personal Bests" records strip). **`marquee` → TROPHY SHELF** (rarer: Mile Club, Strong Season; rank kept OFF the shelf since it's in the card header). **career milestones → TROPHY ROOM** (the 10-trophy Hall at /trophy-room; link added under the card). Root problem this fixed: two parallel systems (awards.ts tiers vs playerCard earnedTrophies/personalBests) diverged — the "Light Footed"/Tight Lines burst pop had NO card home + the shelf MIXED big+small. Now DriverDetailPage's card memo computes `earnedAwards` (with computeGrind streak) once and splits `badges=tier==='burst'` / `shelf=tier==='marquee' && !rank`. PlayerCard props changed bests/trophies→badges/shelf. `earnedTrophies` in playerCard.ts is now DEAD (kept, harmless; personalBests still used BY awards.ts for the burst awards). **Also v1.64.0:** "THIS SEASON"→"LAST SEASON" (it's last 3 complete months); EntityAvatar (shared truck/driver/trailer) collapses its generate controls behind a corner-pencil "Change" once an avatar exists (Done re-collapses); driver avatar 118→150 to fill the card header.**

**TROPHY-EARN CEREMONY POPS SHIPPED v1.65.0 (PR #222, 2026-07-13):** completes the tier ladder — earning a Hall trophy now fires the GRANDEST pop. New top AwardTier `"trophy"` (above recap>marquee>burst); Award gained `image?` (the approved AI art). `earnedTrophyAwards(catalog, statuses, recordsByKey)` in awards.ts maps earned trophies → awards. `useAwardPops` now also fetches getTrophies+getDrivers and runs the SAME `computeAllStatuses(TROPHY_CATALOG, byKey, {lifetimeMiles, driverCount=active, truckCount, cumulativeGross=Σ delivered loadRevenue})` the Hall uses, merging earned trophy awards ahead of the base awards → fire-once via seen store. New `components/comic/TrophyCeremony.tsx`: full-gold screen takeover, confetti storm, shows the trophy `image` (fallback Trophy icon), "TROPHY UNLOCKED", button → /trophy-room; AwardPopHost renders it as the top-priority takeover (trophy ?? recap ?? marquee); newAwards orders trophy first. DEMO_AWARDS gained a trophy demo (no art → icon). **On Jason's prod device the seen-store predates trophies, so his already-earned Owner Operator trophy reads as NEW and pops once on next dashboard load (a built-in first-run reveal) — future earns pop thereafter.** 183 tests. The full award pipeline is now: burst→BADGES(driver card), marquee→TROPHY SHELF, recap-close→RecapCeremony, trophy-earn→TrophyCeremony. STILL QUEUED: per-diem, cash-flow forecast, RLS.**

**DESIGN-FIRST RULE (Jason's, added to CLAUDE.md §2 2026-07-13):** for any feature with a visual/UI surface, show Jason a rendered design mockup (show_widget/artifact) and get approval BEFORE writing code — a hard gate like the verify gate. **PAYOFF TRACKERS + AUTO-EARN TROPHIES SHIPPED v1.66.0 (PR #223, 2026-07-13):** "own it outright" tracker on the truck/trailer ASSET pages (Jason's call — trackers on asset pages, INPUT on the Expenses obligation editor). Migration 036 added to `obligations`: original_balance, current_balance, balance_as_of, payoff_date, asset_type ('truck'|'trailer'), asset_id (nullable, no FK — spans trucks+trailers). `lib/metrics/payoff.ts` computePayoff → %owned=(orig-owed)/orig, paymentsLeft/payoffDate EXACT from payoff_date else estimated at pace (balance÷payment) — chosen because the TRUCK is a Lone Mountain LEASE-purchase (no APR amortization) while the TRAILER is a First Bank of the Lake amortizing loan; simple current-balance model fits both + productizes (user updates balance off a statement, re-anchors). `components/fleet/PayoffTracker.tsx` = road-to-own card (vehicle rides to a FREE & CLEAR flag, gold PAID OFF at $0). ObligationsCard editor expanded with the loan fields (Fragment-wrapped rows). **AUTO-EARN:** status.ts free-and-clear/trailer-paid-off now `loanTrophy(record, loan)` = earned if manual OR loan.paidOff; shows "$Xk to go" progress bar in the Hall; feeds Highway Legend capstone. `assetLoanStatus()` in payoff.ts derives {paidOff,ownedPct,owed} from obligations; wired into BOTH useAwardPops + TrophyHallPage's computeAllStatuses (both added getObligations). When a balance hits $0 → auto-earns → fires TrophyCeremony pop (they already have art). **PROD DATA (from Jason's Drive contracts, seeded):** TRUCK (Lone Mountain lease) orig $69,000 → owed $44,100 (no payoff_date → ~28 payments/~Nov2028 at pace, 36% owned), asset truck 3686d2e6. TRAILER (FBOL loan #105660970, 7.75%, 120mo) orig $39,408.09 → owed $38,298.63 as-of 06/27/2026 (payoff_date 2036-01-27 exact, 3% owned), asset trailer 7c2988b1; **trailer payment CORRECTED $478→$475.19** (contract). Best Egg (personal, ~$11.4k) left as plain obligation (no tracker). 189 tests. STILL QUEUED: per-diem, cash-flow "when money lands", RLS.**

**AWARD SYSTEM REBUILT — 4 TIERS (Phase 1 SHIPPED v1.67.0, PR #229, 2026-07-13):** Jason restructured the old conflated "badges" into FOUR distinct tiers, each with its own MECHANIC + in-theme name (he dislikes "badge"): **PERSONAL BESTS / "Record Book"** = improving records (best week/tank/load/deadhead/most-per-week), compete-with-self, easy, slide-in pop then record updates; **PATCHES** = HARD, STACKABLE ×N feats (re-earn stacks a count), on the driver page patch-board; **MEDALS** = tiered I/II/III honors, worn on the CARD HEADER right of the name (ribboned bronze/silver/gold medallions), earned-only; **TROPHIES** = career monuments (Trophy Room, unchanged). Hierarchy metaphor: patches(collectible) < medals(awarded) < trophies(won). **ADAPTIVE RATCHETING BAR (Jason's key rule, `lib/awards/adaptiveBar.ts` computeStack):** "impressive feat" awards' thresholds are NOT hardcoded — set from the user's OWN history at ~Nth-best-ever (n=5) for the metric = hard-but-attainable; **ratchets HARDER only, never eases** (a good economy raises the bar, a downturn holds it — never scales backward); earns LOCK IN (walk chronologically, earn if value clears the bar-in-effect-at-that-time → ×N count is monotonic, never rolls back). lowerIsBetter flag for deadhead. Cold-start uses a conservative floor until minHistory. **FIXED vs ADAPTIVE split (confirmed):** cumulative MILESTONES stay FIXED universal marks (Mile Club 100k/250k/500k/1M, Freight Hauler 100/250/500, Big Earner $250k/500k/750k net, Iron Streak 4/8/12wk, Debt Crusher 25/50/75% off the payoff tracker, Strong Season) — 1M miles means the same for everyone; only the relative "how-good-was-that" PATCHES adapt. Patches: big-ticket($/load), long-hauler(mi), mountain-mover(weight), rainmaker($/month net), iron-week(loads/wk), clean-run(deadhead% lower-better) = adaptive; trailblazer(distinct states — the ×N IS the 48-state map), coast-to-coast(W↔E states), doubleheader(2+/day) = structural. All derived from load data + asset-scopeable (pass pre-filtered loads → reuse for truck/trailer later). Files: `lib/awards/{adaptiveBar,patches,medals}.ts` (+tests, 12 tests), `components/awards/{awardIcons,MedalBadge,RecordBook,PatchBoard}`; PlayerCard props badges/shelf→medals; DriverDetailPage computes all three. Cut tarp+hazmat patches (too frequent). **DESIGN approved via 2 show_widget mockups first (per design-first rule). Debt Crusher medal hangs off the v1.66 payoff tracker; every medal line ends in a Trophy = one striving ladder.** 201 tests. **PHASE 2 SHIPPED v1.68.0 (PR #234, 2026-07-13):** pop restyle + Guide catalog — the ONE engine now drives card + pops + Guide identically. awards.ts `earnedAwards` REWRITTEN to emit tiers **record/patch/medal** (dropped burst/marquee; imports computePatches/computeMedals + personalBests): record beat→corner slide-in (~3.5s via AwardPopHost timeout, "New record — Top Week"); patch earned/stacked→corner slide-in w/ ×count; medal tier-up→NEW `components/comic/MedalAward.tsx` screen-takeover w/ ribboned medallion in its metal (bronze/silver/gold via medalTier); rank-up = a medal award (medalTier 3). AwardInputs gained `loanPaidPct` (useAwardPops passes max truck/trailer ownedPct → Debt Crusher medal). AwardPopHost: takeover=trophy>recap>medal, slide-ins=patch+record, icons via shared awardIcon() (extended w/ truck/package/stack/users). DELETED dead MarqueeAward.tsx. newAwards order: trophy>recap(grandest)>medal>patch>record. DEMO_AWARDS updated. GuidePage "The Award System" section: 4 tiers explained + full medal ladder + full patch list w/ how-to, from static `PATCH_GUIDE`(patches.ts)/`MEDAL_GUIDE`(medals.ts). AWARD SYSTEM NOW COMPLETE (records/patches/medals/trophies). Also still queued: per-diem, cash-flow "when money lands", RLS.**

**TRUCK PAGE METRICS + TRUCK AWARDS + FLEET COMPARISON SHIPPED v1.69.0 (PR #236, 2026-07-13):** each ASSET gets its OWN achievements (Jason's rule — not the driver's revenue feats recycled). `lib/metrics/truckMetrics.ts` computeTruckMetrics → **utilization = active-weeks % (Jason's pick: distinct weeks-with-a-delivered-load ÷ weeks-since-in-service)**, avg MPG + best tank (fuelStats), fuel/mi, revenue/mi, **cost-to-run/mi = (fuel spend + maintenance spend) ÷ miles** — Jason: label must be CLEAR it's "what the truck cost the owner" → "Cost to run / mi · fuel + maintenance" (the truck NOTE is excluded, it lives in the payoff tracker, not double-counted), mi/month; `fleetSummary()` per-truck rows. Maintenance has no truck_id → scoped by service.unit ('tractor'/'both') + cost (fine for 1 truck). `lib/awards/truckAwards.ts` reuses adaptiveBar + medals.tiered (now exported): PATCHES = feather-foot(tank mpg), iron-horse(month miles), marathon(haul miles), thrifty(tank $/mi lower-better) — all adaptive ratcheting; MEDALS = mile-club(odometer), fuel-miser(avg mpg 6.5/7/7.5), workhorse(loads 100/250/500), debt-crusher(truck payoff); RECORDS = best tank/big month miles/best rev-per-mi/longest haul. TruckDetailPage: medals worn on header (MedalBadge by unit#), metrics strip (Kpi tiles), RecordBook + PatchBoard. TrucksPage: fleet-comparison table (util/mpg/rev-mi/mi-mo per truck, best-per-column green), shown only at 2+ trucks (dormant for Jason's 1 truck). `RecordBook` GENERALIZED to take `RecordChip[]` (+ exported `driverRecordChips`); driver/truck/trailer each feed own set. awardIcons += feather/flag/droplet. 205 tests. **TRAILER PAGE is the natural next step — mostly a new catalog (trailer-flavored awards + trailer metrics), engine + components already reusable.** Also still queued: per-diem, cash-flow "when money lands", RLS.**
2. **METRIC TRANSPARENCY / TRUST (Jason 2026-07-12, high priority):** he wants to SEE the math behind every metric so he can challenge it + confirm correctness; end-users need to TRUST the metrics. **Build a GUIDE section explaining how each metric is derived and WHY** — ideally with GRAPHICAL representations. Surface the formula + inputs, not just the result. (He's caught real errors by challenging the math — this is the whole-app trust layer.) See auto-memory [[metric-transparency-and-trust]].
3. **EXPENSES PAGE UPDATE:** reflect the new booking-ladder / gross-per-mile / net-revenue model. Make the cost KPIs UNAMBIGUOUS — label cost-per-mile as "cost / TOTAL mile" ($3.17, all miles driven) distinct from the booking walk-away ($4.34) and show the chain (cost/mile → ÷keep → book rate; deadhead folded into miles). This was the source of a multi-turn confusion; explicit labels + the derivation would prevent it.
4. **Phase 3 — net-revenue coverage sweep — SHIPPED v1.62.0 (PR #217, 2026-07-13).** Net = money kept (truck/driver/trailer/load-detail-net-line/loads-page $); gross = market + pricing (agent/lane/rate-per-mile/gross-column); pipeline = count. Still queued: trophy-earn → marquee award pop.** **Per Diem tracker** also still queued (own page on the money side; count days-away-from-tax-home → IRS deduction). PASSED FOR NOW (offered, not chosen): #3 settlement reconciliation (heavy but real recovered money — Landstar deduction leaks + days-to-pay/AR aging), #4 oversize permit/escort intel (niche differentiator). **Round 2 greenlit (parked 2026-07-10):** (6) **Own-the-truck payoff tracker** — turn the loan obligation into a finish line: progress bar to owning the truck outright, projected payoff date from revenue pace, "≈N more loads to free-and-clear"; comic truck fills amber → gold PAID OFF stamp. LIGHT, Jason's top round-2 pick. (7) **Cash-flow forecast / "when money lands"** — days-to-pay per broker/agent + AR aging → forward money-incoming calendar (owed $X, ~$Y lands this week). MEDIUM. (10) **Grind meter — SHIPPED v1.51.0 (PR #198), FIXED v1.51.1 (PR #199, 2026-07-11).** Dashboard card: each pay-week (Wed–Tue) graded on **WEEKLY GROSS vs the gross PACE targets** (getGrossTargets weeklyBreakEven/weeklyTarget + getWeekBookedGross) — beat target(green)/covered floor(amber)/below(red)/home(grey, no freight). **v1.51.1 fix: originally graded on rate-per-loaded-mile (rate ladder), which CONTRADICTED the "this week · booked" card above it** (a big-gross/high-mile week read "covering costs" while that card said "beat target"). Now both use gross pace so they agree. `classify(gross, grossTargets)`. Streak = consecutive TARGET-beating weeks (Jason's chosen bar); HOME weeks skipped (rest never breaks it, inferred as zero-delivery weeks — upgrades to real home data when per-diem lands). Card: flame + current streak, personal best, 14-week strip, this-week status, ON FIRE stamp ≥5wk. Milestones 4/8/12wk fire "On a Roll" burst via award pops. `lib/metrics/grind.ts` (computeGrind/classify/currentStreakOf/bestStreakOf, 5 tests) reuses getCostBasis/getRateLadder/getWeekRpm; `hooks/useGrind.ts`; `components/dashboard/GrindMeter.tsx`; awards.ts gained `streak` input + on-a-roll:<mark>; useAwardPops computes grind (all-active obligations for ladder). Real-data check: his weeks are a green-run-then-amber mix, current streak reads low/cold now (best ~5) — honest, target is a high bar. **NOW BUILDING: #8 player card** (see below).

**PLAYER CARD (#8) — DESIGN SPEC (in design as of 2026-07-10, not yet built):** An owner-operator "trading card" for Jason/DTS — the agent prestige system turned inward. **Two-part model:** (a) permanent **career prestige** tier that only climbs — Rookie → Roadrunner → Veteran → Road Captain → Highway Legend (parallel to agentPrestige rookie→legend), seeded from real lifetime miles (truck at 582k = already Veteran/Road Captain) + banked good seasons; (b) **current-season form** (per quarter, can move up/down) = **worst-of two sub-grades**: RPM-vs-your-own-rate-ladder (reuse getRateLadder/getWindowRpm, walkAway/minimum/target/strong) AND net-margin-vs-bands. Worst-of is deliberate (honest + diagnostic — shows which lever to pull); show BOTH sub-grades on the card. **Margin = P&L operating net margin, NET of Landstar** = (income_total − cogs_total − expense_total)/income_total; income_total is ALREADY net of Landstar's commission (verified: only a $2k/6mo "Commissions & fees" line, not the cut). Do NOT subtract truck/loan principal from margin — debt is already in RPM's true-cash break-even (walkAway), avoid double-count. **MARGIN BANDS (LOCKED):** Strong ≥27% · Target 17–27% · Minimum 8–17% · Below <8%. Grounded in his real 6mo (Jan–Jun 2026) net margins: 25.2/34.3/23.3/15.3/34.0/19.8, blended 25.1%; vs industry (owner-op basis, incl. own pay): avg 10–15%, strong 15–25%, excellent 25–35%+. **Card stat line:** miles, net revenue, net profit, loads, avg RPM, deadhead %, best lane, best MPG, biggest load + trophy shelf. **Avatar:** reuse his generated driver avatar_url (monogram fallback). **Lives:** own top-level sidebar page "My Card" under Dashboard. **AWARDS (tiered):** full catalog, two visual tiers — 🎬 Marquee = full animated card-takeover popup on dashboard login (rare/big: rank-ups, mile clubs, truck paid off, record month, Strong season, $1M revenue); minor = a LIGHT frequent treatment (🚫 wax-seal/envelope-seal REJECTED twice — do not revisit; deciding among comic POW-burst / ink-splat / mini-trading-card / neon-marquee per the ADULT-COMIC/Roger-Rabbit tone — see [[suggest-comic-theme-opportunities]]). **Jason's award principle: he most values RE-EARNABLE self-competition awards** (beat your own best / clear a recurring bar) over one-time milestones. Catalog incl. re-earnable (Personal Best Week, new best MPG "Feather Foot", Wide Load [widest yet], most loads/wk, best RPM load, lowest deadhead "Tight Lines", best margin, biggest load, streaks) + one-time (mile clubs, $1M, truck paid off, Century) + **Relationship Builder** (Jason's idea — Nth load with the same agent, re-earnable per agent). "Wide Load"/"Super Load" needs a NUMERIC width field on loads (dimensions is free-text string today) — small add, compounds with oversize-permit idea #4. **SEEN vs EARNED architecture (Jason caught the muddle — get this right):** EARNED (rank/badges) = computed live from data now, NO backend table needed for card+pops; a small backend achievements ledger (earned_at only, SHARED) is deferred to the grind-meter/recap phase. SEEN ("已celebrated?", decides if a pop fires) = per-DEVICE localStorage, NEVER backend — because **Jason + Brandie SHARE the one DTS login**, so a shared "seen" flag would let whoever-logs-in-first rob the other of the pop; per-device makes both see each pop once (first device-load silently baselines current earned set → no day-one flood). Adding the backend earned-ledger later does NOT reintroduce the problem (it tracks earned, never seen). **Build phasing:** Phase 1 = card page (rank + season grade + stat line + trophy shelf + avatar); Phase 2 = celebration layer (award engine + marquee popup + minor treatment + per-device seen). **MINOR-AWARD TREATMENT DECIDED:** three-tier system — 🎬 marquee big-card takeover (rare), comic **POW-burst** (frequent re-earnable pops, `ComicBurst` primitive), **mini trading cards** = the trophy shelf collection (`MiniTrophyCard`). Bangers font. **PLACEMENT DECIDED:** NOT a separate page — the player card IS the hauling driver's page header (driver page also keeps records section + trophy shelf; CDL stays read-only; non-hauling drivers keep the plain page). If a 2nd hauling driver is ever added, lift business-level bits (margin/RPM/prestige) to the dashboard. **PHASE 1 SHIPPED — v1.47.0 (PR #192 merged 2026-07-10, Vercel auto-deploy; frontend-only). Jason verifies visually on prod.** New: `lib/constants/playerCard.ts` (RANK_TIERS mile thresholds Rookie/100k/300k/500k/1M, MARGIN_BANDS 27/17/8), `lib/metrics/playerCard.ts` (careerRank, marginGrade, rpmGrade, worseGrade, getSeasonStats, personalBests, earnedTrophies — 10 tests), `components/comic/ComicBurst.tsx` + `MiniTrophyCard.tsx`, `components/playercard/PlayerCard.tsx`, Bangers font in index.html + `--font-comic` in index.css, DriverDetailPage rewritten (card header for hauling driver). **REAL-DATA FINDINGS (prod):** all 56 loads driver-assigned to Jason (card renders); windowRPM gross $6.30/loaded mi, biggest load $10,065, best week $14,086; **deadhead reads 0% — deadhead_miles not entered on his loads (real, not a bug)**; given true-cost break-even (~$4.72, incl obligations incl $1000 distribution), RPM sub-grade ≈ Minimum → season Form ≈ Minimum (honest, not the mock's Target). Season stat "Miles" falls back to loaded+deadhead when odometer absent. **v1.47.1 (PR #193): added "THIS SEASON · <range> · last 3 complete months" heading over the stat line (timeframe was unclear).** **v1.48.0 (PR #194): True Net + metric colors.** Card headline profit = **True Net = operating profit − active DEBT obligations** (owner DRAWS excluded — a draw is profit taken, not a cost). Stat line = waterfall: net revenue → operating profit → true net ("what you keep, before draw"). **Grades + awards stay on OPERATING P&L (immutable) → toggling any obligation can NEVER fire a false award / change a grade (closes the hide/unhide gaming hole Jason flagged).** Obligations gained `is_draw` flag (migration `031_obligation_draw.sql`, applied dev+prod; prod Distribution backfilled is_draw=true; Truck Note/Trailer/Best Egg = debt). Backend obligationServices returns/accepts is_draw; ObligationsCard has per-row owner-draw toggle (HandCoins). Draws stay in break-even/rate math (getCostBasis uses ALL active incl draw) but drop from True Net (getSeasonStats new `obligationsDebtMonthly` param = active non-draw). Q2: $79.3k rev → $18.8k op profit → **$11.5k true net** (−$2,411/mo loans×3; $1k draw excluded), op-margin grade 23.7% (relabeled "Op margin"). Metric COLORS: avg RPM off rate ladder, deadhead off DEADHEAD_TARGET(0.2), profit by sign, records green; volume stats neutral. 154 tests. **PHASE 2 SHIPPED — v1.49.0 (PR #195, 2026-07-10; frontend-only).** Celebration layer on the DASHBOARD. `lib/metrics/awards.ts` (3 tests): `earnedAwards(inputs)` → Award[] {id,tier,name,detail,icon}; **STABLE ids that change only on genuine re-earn** (best-week:<value>, mileclub:<marker>, rank:<key>, relationship:<agent>:<mark>, strong-season:<label>, century:<n>) so beating a record re-pops, unchanged stays quiet; `newAwards(earned, seenSet)` (marquee-first order); `DEMO_AWARDS` for preview. `hooks/useAwardPops.ts`: fetches periods/fuel/trucks/obligations, computes earned, diffs vs **per-DEVICE localStorage** `dash.awards.v1` {baselined, seen[]} — SEEN is never backend (Jason+Brandie share login → per-device lets both catch each pop once); **first device-load silently baselines** (no flood). Components `components/comic/`: `MarqueeAward` (full-screen takeover: card fly-in + shine + ray-burst + confetti, "LET'S ROLL" dismiss — rare/big: rank-up/mileclub/strong-season), `BurstAward` (corner toast, ComicBurst press-in, auto-fade 5s — frequent re-earnable), `AwardPopHost` (marquee blocks bursts until dismissed; icon map). Keyframes (award-pop/shine/spin/fall/press/toast-in) in index.css. Wired into DashboardPage. **PREVIEW: dashboard `?awarddemo`** shows sample marquee→bursts (real device baselines silently so no pop without earning). 157 tests. **AGENTS RE-SKIN DONE — v1.50.0 (PR #196, 2026-07-10):** prestige bursts heavier ink, RatingStamp + prestige/rank labels → Bangers, TrophyCase → noir card + halftone (matches player-card shelf); rating medallion list chips left clean. ### dash — prior version 1.44.0 (fuel page chunk 2: EIA national diesel + form auto-calc 2026-07-10)

**FUEL PAGE CHUNK 2 — SHIPPED v1.44.0 (PR #188, 2026-07-10; backend + frontend, NO migration).** (a) **EIA national diesel price**: backend proxy `GET /fuel/national-diesel` (`backend/src/services/fuelPriceService.js` + route in `fuelEntryRoutes.js` — MUST sit BEFORE `/:fuel_entry_id` or it's read as an id) calls EIA v2 petroleum server-side so the key never hits the browser; **narrowed to ONE series** `EMD_EPD2D_PTE_NUS_DPG` (weekly U.S. No 2 Diesel retail $/gal) via facets `duoarea=NUS`/`product=EPD2D`/`process=PTE` + `length=1` (NOT the 5000-row firehose Jason's raw URL pulled); `value` arrives as a STRING → coerce; in-memory cache 12h (feed updates weekly Mon); fails SOFT → returns `{diesel:null}` (HTTP 200) so page never breaks. Pure `parseNationalDiesel(json)` (`json.response.data[0]`) unit-verified + live-verified (2026-07-06 = $4.578/gal). Frontend `components/fuel/DieselCompareCard.tsx` (national avg + week vs your blended cost/gal + delta; **comic "Under the national" `<Stamp color=#4ade80>` when you beat it** — Jason runs ~$4.16 vs $4.58 national). `getNationalDiesel()` in fuelService, `NationalDiesel` type, fetched best-effort in its own effect. (b) **FORM AUTO-CALC**: add-fill-up form now takes **Total $** and computes **price/gal = total ÷ gallons** (3-decimal, live) — the two numbers on the receipt — instead of asking price/gal directly (Jason's request). **KEY HANDLING**: EIA key written to `backend/.env` (gitignored, verified `git check-ignore`); NEVER echoed/committed. **⚠️ DEPLOY TODO: set `EIA_API_KEY` in RAILWAY Variables for prod** — until then the endpoint returns null + the compare card just hides (no breakage). Vercel auto-deploys frontend, Railway auto-deploys backend from main. Backend has NO test runner → verified via node inline import + live probe (throwaway, deleted). 131 frontend tests green.

Page `FuelEntriesPage.tsx` (route `/fuel-entries`): 6 KPIs (Avg MPG, Cost/mile, Cost/gal, Avg weekly·90d, Total gallons, Total spend), toggle add-fill-up form (date/odo/gallons/price/vendor/city/state; single-truck auto-resolves; state uppercased; "120+ = full" hint), `MpgChart` (recharts line, amber, one pt/full) + `WeeklyCostChart` (recharts bar + dashed 90d-avg ReferenceLine), fill-up log (odo-desc, Full green/Partial amber pills, per-tank MPG, delete). `types/fuelEntry.ts` + `services/fuelService.ts` (coerces numeric strings). **17 real Fuelly fills SEEDED TO DEV** (user a0…01, odo 570368–582450): 9 full/8 partial, 1975.33 gal, $8213.42 spend, $4.16/gal, avg MPG 6.55 — metrics cross-checked vs dev SQL, all reconcile. **PROD LOADED 2026-07-10** — same 17 fills inserted into PROD (user 2fe59f51-155c-4a18-b9e1-1d8a30cfc59d, truck 3686d2e6-98f6-415c-b41f-d89bc2bc2437 unit 580991) via idempotent NOT-EXISTS-on-(user,odometer) guard; prod totals match dev exactly (17/9/8, 1975.33 gal, $8213.42, $4.158/gal). ## TRIPS ARC — DETAILED STATE

**Model decided (Option B):** A non-revenue trip = `trip_type: 'deadhead'`, `trip_source: 'user'`, `status: 'completed'`. Loads untouched. `tripStops` and `trip_type='revenue'` stay dormant for a future full-model arc.

**trips table (EXISTS in prod):** trip_id (uuid PK), user_id (FK), truck_id (FK, nullable), driver_id (FK, nullable), trip_type (enum revenue|deadhead), trip_source (enum user|system), trip_date (date), status (enum planned|active|completed|cancelled, default planned), odometer_start/end (int4, nullable, CHECK >=0), is_estimated (bool, default true), trip_number (int4, UNIQUE, auto via `nextval('trips_trip_number_seq')`), created_at/updated_at.

**Issue #1 — backend fixes: DONE + tested.**

- `createTrip` now sets trip_type='deadhead', trip_source='user', status='completed'; `is_estimated` added to allowedFields.
- getTrips/getTrip: inner JOINs → LEFT JOINs (nullable truck/driver — else null-FK trips vanish).
- `.http` verified: deadhead trip inserts, auto-numbers, lists (even with null truck+driver).

**Issue #2 — frontend data layer: essentially DONE (confirm merged).**

- `Trip` type (types/trip.ts) — correct nullability, enum literal unions.
- `TripInput` type (types/tripInput.ts) — FIX APPLIED: odometers are `number` (not string), fields optional (`?:`) except trip_date.
- `getTrips` + `createTrip` services (tripsService.ts) — extract `response.data.trips` / `.trip`.
- `useTrips(refreshKey)` hook — FIX APPLIED: `setIsLoading(true)` + `setError(null)` at start of each fetch (was stuck false / stale error on refetch).

**Trip categories contract — DONE on dev (2026-07-07), NOT yet on prod.** Migration `023_add_trip_purpose.sql` (CREATE TYPE trip_purpose enum + DELETE test rows + ADD COLUMN NOT NULL) ran clean on dev. Types (`Trip`, `TripInput`) carry `trip_purpose` required/non-null. New `TripForm.tsx` (date, purpose select, odo start [prefilled], odo end, is_estimated Estimated/Actual select). Wired into `TripsPage.tsx` with a "Log Trip" toggle + list refresh on success.

Trips feature DONE. Next: wire trips into the deadhead metric (now the active arc). **Trips was a CONFIRMED must-have for v1. Jason opted out of coding THIS feature (instructor builds it — teaching-mode exception, like the dashboard rush). Plan: build → test on dev → ship. No keep/cut question.**

**ODOMETER MODEL (clarified 2026-07-07 — corrects a prior misread by the instructor):** `getDeadheadPercent` (`frontend/src/lib/metrics/dashboard.ts:70`) is computed PER LOAD: `Σ(odometer_end − odometer_start) − Σ(loaded_miles)` over delivered loads that have both odometers. It is NOT gap-inferred between consecutive loads. Consequence: empty miles NOT tied to fetching a load (home/shop/personal/repositioning) currently FALL THROUGH — a real, uncounted gap. Trips fill that gap by carrying their OWN `odometer_start/end`. Loads + trips must TILE the odometer line (each segment's start = prior segment's end; no gaps, no overlaps) or deadhead double-counts. Wiring trips INTO the deadhead metric stays deferred (backlog), but capture odometers now so that wiring is later a pure calc change (no migration/backfill).

**DEADHEAD vs REPOSITIONING (Jason's vocabulary — governs, do NOT conflate):** *deadhead* = per-load empty miles to reach the next load's pickup (the odo-delta-minus-loaded above); automatic, lives on the load, NEVER a trip — cost forced by the load taken. *repositioning* = empty miles driven to a BETTER MARKET with NO booked load being fetched; speculative; REQUIRES a logged trip to capture the odo gap; is one of the four categories.

**ODOMETER PREFILL (must-have this version — data protection; Brandie also inputs):** create-trip form auto-prefills `odometer_start` from the truck's LATEST recorded odometer = `MAX(odometer_end)` across BOTH loads AND trips (NOT just the last load's drop — else back-to-back repositioning trips overlap). RESOLVED (2026-07-08): sourced via a small backend endpoint `GET /trips/latest-odometer` (authoritative in SQL; the trips page has no loads client-side to derive from).

**dash — stack & locations:**

- Backend: Express 5 + Postgres (Supabase); two projects (prod + dts-dash-dev). Local: `~/projects/dash/backend/`. `db` from `db/pool.js` (`db.query`; `db.pool.connect()` for transactions).
